### [73] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowEdit框架，通过在潜在空间学习发音修正并使用现代Hopfield网络存储，实现了对冻结流匹配TTS模型的终身适应，在多语言专有名词基准上将音素错误率降低了92.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配文本转语音系统在零样本质量方面表现出色，但在部署后保持静态：除非重新训练模型，否则对未登录词汇中专有名词的发音错误将持续存在。

Method: 引入FlowEdit终身适应框架，学习发音修正作为潜在条件编辑而非权重更新；当提供纠正反馈时，在文本嵌入空间优化标记级扰动，然后将修正存储在作为内容可寻址情景记忆的现代Hopfield网络中；推理时通过带相似性门的软注意力检索修正，实现模糊形态匹配。

Result: 在包含18个语系312个多语言专有名词的基准测试上，相对于零样本基线，FlowEdit将目标词音素错误率降低了92.7%，同时保持相同的通用语音质量；在单个GPU上约15秒完成修正。

Conclusion: FlowEdit成功实现了对冻结流匹配TTS模型的终身适应，能够高效纠正发音错误而无需重新训练，为部署后的TTS系统提供了一种有效的持续学习解决方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [74] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DeepSWIP，一种针对DeepProbLog程序的单世界反事实语义系统，通过神经物化和加权模型计数实现精确的反事实推理，相比DeepTwin方法获得2.14倍加速，并解决了神经校准退化导致的偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号系统（如DeepProbLog）结合了神经感知和概率逻辑，但其标准推理仅支持关联推理。反事实推理需要因果语义来处理干预和证据，而现有方法存在计算效率低、神经校准敏感性和稀有证据不稳定等问题。

Method: 引入DeepSWIP单世界反事实语义：1）通过神经物化将固定上下文神经谓词简化为普通ProbLog选择；2）应用单世界干预程序(SWIPs)；3）通过单个变换程序的加权模型计数(WMC)计算反事实。在有限基化和唯一支持模型假设下，DeepSWIP相对于学习到的物化FCM是精确的。

Result: 在MPI3D数据集上对12,000个查询的实验验证：1）与DeepTwin构造相比，变换正确性得到确认；2）避免Twin的内生复制带来2.14倍推理加速；3）SUMO HOV实验显示神经校准退化会偏置插件估计，而正确范围的随机化策略AIPW估计器消除了总体均值和ATE估计量的大部分一阶偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP为DeepProbLog提供了精确且高效的单世界反事实推理框架，通过商WMC形式识别活跃神经概率，解释了干预清理、校准敏感性和稀有证据不稳定现象，并通过合适的估计器设计缓解了神经校准问题带来的偏差。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [75] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了LedgerAgent方法，通过独立账本显式维护任务状态并在工具调用前检查策略约束，显著提升了客服领域工具调用代理的性能和策略依从性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用代理在客服领域面临两个主要问题：一是任务状态管理隐式化导致代理可能基于过时、缺失或错误信息做决策；二是语法有效的工具调用可能违反依赖当前任务状态的领域策略。

Method: 提出LedgerAgent推理时方法，维护独立账本显式记录观察到的任务状态（相关事实、标识符、约束和条件），将状态渲染到提示中，并在执行改变环境的工具调用前检查状态依赖的策略约束，阻止违规操作。

Result: 在四个客服领域和包含开源及闭源模型的混合测试集上，相比标准基于提示的工具调用方法，LedgerAgent在平均pass^k指标上取得提升，在更严格的多轮一致性指标下提升最大。

Conclusion: 通过显式状态管理和策略检查机制，LedgerAgent有效解决了工具调用代理的状态管理问题，提高了策略依从性和任务完成质量。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [76] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次将DAAM框架适配到语音扩散模型，通过跨注意力归因分析揭示风格描述文本中各词元对生成语音声学特征的影响机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格描述文本转语音系统使用自然语言控制声音特性，但单个词汇如何影响声学输出尚不明确。理解这一点对于诊断失败模式和提升表现力TTS的可控性至关重要。

Method: 提出跨注意力归因方法，首次将DAAM框架适配到语音领域，应用于CapSpeech-TTS模型。方法提取25层和24个ODE步骤的每个词元热力图，分析3,600个（风格描述，文本转录）组合，包括120个风格描述词条件生成30个文本转录。

Result: 发现：(1)风格词元的时间方差低于内容/功能词元，证实全局条件作用；(2)风格注意力与F0和能量相关；(3)风格条件在早期步骤和深层达到峰值；(4)注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，与风格重要性峰值同时出现，表明网络在最关键风格阶段的选择性最大化。

Conclusion: 这是首个研究自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型中跨注意力的工作，为理解和改进风格描述TTS系统的可控性提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [77] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合专家模型在分布偏移下的校准行为，证明了专家校准对硬路由模型充分但对软路由模型不足，并提出对抗重加权方法来改善整体校准效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准对理解和信任模型预测概率至关重要，近期研究表明专家级校准能提升集成模型的准确性和校准性，但校准在何种条件下对MoE模型有效尚不清楚，特别是在分布偏移场景下路由机制与专家校准的交互作用缺乏深入理解。

Method: 研究硬路由和软路由MoE模型在分布偏移下的行为，分析专家级校准对整体模型校准的充分性；针对软路由模型的校准不足问题，提出对抗重加权方法，通过惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合的校准误差来优化准确率-校准权衡。

Result: 证明了专家校准对硬路由模型在广泛分布偏移类别下能确保整体模型校准，但对软路由模型不充分；所提对抗重加权方法在多种模型类别、预测任务和分布偏移下均能改善准确率-校准权衡，在平均表现和困难数据子集上均有提升。

Conclusion: 专家校准对MoE模型的作用取决于路由机制类型，软路由模型需要额外机制来保证整体校准；对抗重加权方法能有效解决这一问题，为分布偏移下MoE模型的可靠概率预测提供了理论与实践指导。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [Certified Euclidean-Residue Minimal-Alignment Switch Decompositions for Three Edge-Disjoint Hamiltonian Cycles in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19832)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部切换演算的构造方法，为非互质Eisenstein-Jacobi网络构建三个边不相交哈密顿圈，其中前两个圈使用最少数量的切换操作，第三个圈作为剩余边补集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eisenstein-Jacobi网络是六度商格互连网络。当生成器参数a,b的最大公约数d>1时，每个单位方向分裂为d个圈，边不相交哈密顿圈问题转化为圈拼接问题。现有方法使用矩形表示和交换调度证明存在性，但缺乏紧致的公式化构造。

Method: 在自然Cayley几何中开发局部切换演算方法。前两个哈密顿圈各使用最少数量的d-1个组件间切换构造，第三个因子作为未使用边的补集获得。证明分离四个要素：组件标签坍缩、锚点取消、提升切换代表的非碰撞性、连通补集关联。所有无限族定理均通过代数补集关联证书证明，而非有限见证或计算枚举。

Result: 为欧几里得剩余族提供了紧致、公式驱动、最小切换的分解方案。前两个哈密顿圈各仅需d-1个组件间切换操作，第三个圈作为补集自动获得。通过符号证明验证了补集关联性质。

Conclusion: 本文贡献不在于证明所有非互质EJ网络的新的存在性定理，而是针对特定参数范围内的欧几里得剩余族，提供了紧凑的公式化最小切换分解，其补集关联性通过代数证书严格证明。表格和CSV数据仅用于验证和复现公式，不作为无限族定理的证明依据。

Abstract: Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six quotient-lattice interconnection networks. For a generator $α=a+bρ$, let $N=a^2+ab+b^2$ and $d=\gcd(a,b)$. If $d=1$, the three natural unit directions already give three edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. If $d>1$, each unit direction splits into $d$ cycles and the EDHC problem becomes a cycle-splicing problem. Existing non-coprime EJ decompositions prove existence by using a rectangular representation and exchange schedules. This paper develops a different, local switch calculus in the natural Cayley geometry. The first two Hamiltonian cycles are built using the minimum possible $d-1$ intercomponent switches each, and the third factor is obtained as the unused edge complement. The contribution is deliberately not a new existence theorem for all non-coprime EJ networks; rather, it is a compact, formula-driven, minimal-switch decomposition for Euclidean-residue families whose complement incidence is proved symbolically. The proof separates four ingredients: component-label collapse, anchor cancellation, noncollision of lifted switch representatives, and connected complement incidence. No infinite-family theorem in this manuscript is proved by finite witnesses or by computational enumeration. The theorem scope is stated for the parameter ranges where an algebraic complement-incidence certificate is written down. Tables and CSV data are used only to verify and reproduce the formulas, never as proof of an infinite-family theorem.

</details>


### [79] [Fault-Tolerant Shared-Relay Communication in Circulant Interconnection Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19833)
*Bader Albader,Galal Hassan,Mohamed R. Al-Mulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向循环网络中两跳原语的故障容错能力，发现生成器的选择对最坏情况下的中继存活率有决定性影响：优化的阈值设计在接近计数下界的范围内实现了中继故障容错，而标准的区间生成器即使在更大的度数下也可能结构性地失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环互连网络具有对称寻址、紧凑的生成器描述和均匀的局部连通性。然而，在有向循环网络中实现故障容错的两跳原语时，如何在给定的节点数和度预算下最大化最坏情况下的共享中继复用度R(n,m)是一个关键问题。目前缺乏系统性的网络设计框架来评估和优化这种容错能力。

Method: 1. 将问题映射为度-冗余景观，定义参数R(n,m)和D_f(n)；2. 利用循环差分-多重性条件作为数学工具；3. 提出中继表预处理和查找算法；4. 对区间循环网络给出负面定理；5. 提供对抗性故障和随机故障的保证；6. 进行精确的小n校准和软件微基准测试；7. 对526,539个生成器集进行可重现研究。

Result: 生成器的选择对于最坏情况下的中继存活率至关重要：优化的阈值设计能够在计数下界的约1.16-1.63倍范围内实现f-中继故障容错，而标准的区间生成器即使在更大的度数下也可能结构性地失效。这表明通过合理的生成器设计，可以在接近理论最优的范围内实现故障容错。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个完整的网络设计框架，证明了生成器选择对循环网络故障容错能力的关键作用。优化设计能够接近理论下界实现中继故障容错，为实际网络设计提供了指导。框架包括多种分析工具和大量实验验证，为未来的网络容错设计研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Circulant interconnection networks provide symmetric addressing, compact generator descriptions, and uniform local connectivity. This paper maps a degree--redundancy landscape for a fault-tolerant two-hop primitive in directed circulants: given $n$ nodes and degree budget $m$, how large can the worst-case shared-relay multiplicity $R(n,m)$ be? A node is a shared relay for an ordered terminal pair if it has outgoing links to both terminals; an $f$-relay-fault-tolerant circulant requires at least $f+1$ such relays for every pair. The underlying feasibility condition is a cyclic difference-multiplicity condition, which we use as a mathematical tool rather than claim as a new object. The contribution is the network-design framework around this tool: the parameters $R(n,m)$ and $D_f(n)$, a negative theorem for interval circulants, relay-table preprocessing and lookup algorithms, adversarial and random failure guarantees, load-balance scope, certified upper-bound interpretation of heuristic designs, exact small-$n$ calibration, a software lookup-versus-search microbenchmark, and a reproducible study of 526,539 generator sets. The results show that generator choice critically determines worst-case relay survivability: optimized threshold designs achieve $f$-relay-fault tolerance within about $1.16$--$1.63$ of the counting lower bound, while standard interval generators can fail structurally even at much larger degrees.

</details>


### [80] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19834)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对稠密Eisenstein-Jacobi网络提出了一种名为EJ-MOEM的非冗余一对多广播修复方法，证明了单故障和双故障情况下的深度界限定理，并通过大规模实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究稠密EJ网络中的非冗余一对多广播修复问题。当网络中存在故障节点时，如何在保持原始健康树组件完整性的前提下，通过最少的修复边将消息传播到所有健康节点，是一个重要且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出EJ-MOEM（多方向边最小修复）方法：评估固定大小的六边形广播树方向族，选择故障感知的候选方向，将故障修剪树收缩为健康组件，并使用外部组件交叉修复边重新连接这些组件。通过三带表示法、扇区后缀附着引理、非相邻扇区分离引理和六方向屏蔽分类等技术进行理论证明。

Result: 证明对于选定的方向（其故障修剪组件图连通），恰好需要c-1条外部修复边（c为健康组件数量）；证明深度证书定理：单故障修复深度不超过t+1，双故障修复深度不超过t+2。通过t=2至18的穷举验证（t=18时达525,825种双故障配置）、t=30的结构化测试和t=200的大规模随机测试，均达到100%成功率。

Conclusion: EJ-MOEM方法成功实现了稠密EJ网络中的非冗余一对多广播修复，生成的结构是健康子图的根生成树，保证每个健康节点恰好接收一次消息，不使用任何故障节点，并保持原始健康树组件不变。理论分析和大规模实验验证了方法的正确性和有效性。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection networks whose finite quotient geometry is naturally represented by a hexagonal axial-coordinate ball. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense EJ network generated by $α=(t+1)+tω$, where $t$ is the network diameter. We propose EJ-MOEM, a multi-orientation edge-minimum repair method that evaluates a constant-size family of hexagonal broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware candidate, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects these components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph: every healthy node receives the message exactly once, no faulty node is used, and the original healthy tree components are preserved. We prove that, for a chosen orientation whose fault-pruned component graph is connected, exactly $c-1$ external repair edges are necessary and sufficient, where $c$ is the number of healthy components. We also prove a depth-certificate theorem for EJ coordinate-reduction trees: every one-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+1$, and every two-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+2$. The proof uses the three-strip representation of EJ hexagons, a sector-suffix attachment lemma, a non-adjacent-sector separation lemma, and a six-direction shielding classification for paired cuts. Extended validation includes exhaustive one- and two-fault enumeration for $t=2,\ldots,12,14,16,18$ (up to $N=1027$ and 525,825 two-fault placements at $t=18$), structured theorem-critical tests through $t=30$, and large random tests through $t=200$, all with 100\% success and no violation of the theorem.

</details>


### [81] [A Topos-Theoretic Interpretation of Blockchain Systems: Sheaves of Consensus and the Logic of Decentralized Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19519)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出使用拓扑斯理论作为区块链系统的新型数学建模语言，以解决传统有限状态机模型无法恰当形式化共识过程的核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的区块链和智能合约形式化模型主要基于经典计算理论（如有限状态机或标记转换系统），但这些模型将区块链最核心、最困难的特征——去中心化环境下的共识达成——视为复杂的实现细节而排除在形式化模型之外。然而共识过程并非辅助功能，而是区块链计算现象的本质特征，需要新的数学语言来忠实建模。

Method: 采用拓扑斯理论，即层范畴理论，作为形式化建模的数学框架。该方法基于拓扑斯理论处理局部一致性和全局真理构建的能力，将其作为区块链系统的原生数学语言。

Result: 提出拓扑斯理论能够为区块链系统提供恰当的形式化建模框架，能够原生地表达由局部一致性定义的系统以及全局真理的构建过程，从而将共识机制纳入形式化模型本身。

Conclusion: 拓扑斯理论是区块链系统形式化的自然数学语言，能够弥补传统计算理论模型的不足，为理解区块链系统的本质特征——去中心化共识——提供了恰当的理论基础。

Abstract: The predominant formal models for blockchain systems, particularly smart contracts, have largely been drawn from the classical theory of computation, with the finite state machine (FSM) or labeled transition system serving as the primary conceptual tool. However, the FSM relegates the most difficult and novel aspect of a blockchain -- the achievement of consensus in a decentralized environment -- to a complex, often messy, implementation detail that lies outside the formal model itself. But the process of consensus is not an ancillary feature; it is the very essence of the computational phenomenon. To model it faithfully, a new mathematical language is required. The central thesis of this work is that topos theory, the theory of categories of sheaves, provides the native mathematical language for systems defined by local consistency and the construction of global truth.

</details>


### [82] [The Sheaf Laplacian: A Topological Framework for Data Fusion and Consensus in Distributed Sensing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19529)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 传统简单图网络模型不足以描述现代分布式系统，需采用层论（Sheaf Theory）作为新的数学语言，其中层拉普拉斯算子可用于分布式感知网络的数据融合与共识建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式系统具有异构智能体、高维多模态数据流以及复杂的上下文依赖关系等特征，而传统基于简单图的经典网络模型表达能力有限，无法充分捕捉这些复杂特性，亟需一种具有更强表达能力的数学语言。

Method: 引入层论（Sheaf Theory）作为描述复杂网络系统的数学框架，并研究层拉普拉斯算子在分布式感知网络中的具体应用。

Result: 证明了层论能够提供比传统图模型更强的表达能力，并且层拉普拉斯算子是实现分布式感知网络中数据融合与共识建立的合适机制。

Conclusion: 层论为现代分布式系统提供了一个强大且合适的数学语言，能够克服传统网络模型的固有局限性，为复杂系统的建模与分析开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We argue here that traditional network models, which are overwhelmingly based on the mathematical construct of a simple graph, are fundamentally insufficient for capturing the complexity of modern distributed systems. Such systems are characterized by heterogeneous agents with diverse capabilities, high-dimensional and multi-modal data streams, and intricate, context-dependent relationships that cannot be adequately described by a simple connection or a scalar weight. The limitations of these classical models necessitate a new mathematical language, one with far greater expressive power. We have found that sheaf theory provides us with such a language. Moreover, we show that the sheaf Laplacian is a suitable mechanism for data fusion and establishing consensus within distributed sensing networks.

</details>


### [83] [SAC: Disaggregated KV Cache System for Sparse Attention LLMs with CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19746)
*Ruiyang Ma,Teng Ma,Junru Li,Hantian Zha,Xuchun Shang,Qingda Hu,Zheng Liu,Xinjun Yang,Tao Ma,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SAC，首个面向稀疏注意力模型的高效分解式KV缓存系统，利用CXL技术实现按需细粒度获取，相比RDMA方案显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型向长上下文推理扩展，内存容量成为主要瓶颈。现有RDMA分解式内存方案需在解码前粗粒度获取整个前缀KV缓存，而稀疏注意力模型在解码时仅使用小部分KV条目，导致严重的传输瓶颈和本地内存浪费。

Method: 提出SAC系统，利用Compute Express Link (CXL)的低延迟、缓存行粒度加载/存储语义，在推理过程中按需获取所需的top-k KV条目，实现细粒度的KV缓存访问。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.2上使用SGLang评估，SAC相比RDMA基准实现2.1倍更高吞吐量、9.7倍更低TTFT（首个token时间）和1.8倍更低TBT（token间时间）。

Conclusion: 基于CXL的分解式架构是新兴稀疏注意力模型的优选基础设施，相比传统RDMA方案具有显著性能优势，为长上下文LLM推理提供了更高效的内存解决方案。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs toward long-context inference has shifted the primary serving system bottleneck from computation to memory capacity. Traditional solutions for dense attention models rely on RDMA-based disaggregated memory pools, which perform coarse-grained fetching of the entire prefix KV cache from remote storage to local memory before decoding. However, this approach is fundamentally inefficient for emerging sparse attention models. While only a small fraction of KV entries are active during decoding, these systems still fetch the full KV cache locally, leading to severe transmission bottlenecks and local memory wastage. To address this, we propose SAC, the first efficient disaggregated KV cache system optimized for sparse attention models. By leveraging the low-latency, cache-line granularity load/store semantics of Compute Express Link (CXL), SAC fetches only the required top-k KV entries on demand during inference. Evaluations on DeepSeek-V3.2 using SGLang show that SAC achieves 2.1x higher throughput, 9.7x lower TTFT, and 1.8x lower TBT compared to RDMA-based baselines, establishing CXL-based disaggregation as the superior infrastructure for emerging sparse attention models.

</details>


### [84] [EVM Workloads in the Wild: Evidence for Multi-Dimensional Gas Metering, State Growth, Delayed Execution, and Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19869)
*Lioba Heimbach,Kushal Babel,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证测量发现，EVM区块链的资源配置并不稳定：存储与计算的比例在链间差异显著，且46%的Base交易和13.9%的以太坊交易在不同历史状态下执行时Gas消耗存在波动，为多维Gas定价和状态增长定价提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EVM区块链Gas计费机制假设执行条件是稳定的：资源混合比例足够恒定以支持单一标量定价，且提交与执行间的状态漂移不会实质性改变交易结果。然而这一假设的有效性尚未得到验证，亟需实证测量来评估其失效程度。

Method: 对2025年以太坊(L1)和Base(L2)进行链上追踪级别测量，每日每链采样3,000个区块；将每笔交易分解为操作码级执行Gas、固有Gas、退款和持久状态增量；在2025年9月的历史状态上重新执行交易，测量Gas使用量和存储访问模式的变化。

Result: 资源混合极不稳定：Base的存储读取和计算分别占执行Gas的29.2%和24.3%，而以太坊存储写入占34.9%；以太坊2025年Gas上限翻倍后向计算密集型转变；Base冷存储读取比例(49.7%)高于以太坊(39.6%)；持久状态增长达456GB(以太坊仅38GB)。执行结果同样不稳定：46.0%的Base交易和13.9%的以太坊交易在不同历史状态下的Gas估算存在差异，MEV和DeFi活动尤为敏感。

Conclusion: 实证结果为多维Gas计费和显式状态增长定价提供了理论基础，揭示状态敏感的执行行为使工作负载估计复杂化，直接影响交易可预测性和用户体验，表明现有单一维度Gas模型存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Gas metering on EVM-compatible blockchains assumes that execution conditions are stable: that the resource mix is constant enough to justify collapsing execution costs into a single scalar with fixed relative prices, and that state drift between submission and execution does not materially alter a transaction's outcome. We measure the extent to which this assumption fails.
  We present a trace-level measurement study of EVM workloads on Ethereum (L1) and Base (L2) throughout 2025, sampling 3,000 blocks per day per chain. We decompose each transaction into opcode-level execution gas, intrinsic gas, refunds, and persistent state deltas. To measure state sensitivity, we re-execute transactions from September 2025 on older states and record how gas usage and storage access patterns change.
  We find the resource mix to be far from stable: on Base, storage reads and compute account for 29.2% and 24.3% of execution gas, while Ethereum devotes 34.9% to storage writes. Ethereum's gas limit doubling during 2025 shifted its own profile toward compute-heavier, Base-like patterns. Base also exhibits a higher fraction of cold storage reads (49.7% versus 39.6% on Ethereum). Persistent state growth, a permanent cost priced as a transient one, reaches 456 GB on Base versus 38 GB on Ethereum.
  Execution outcomes are equally unstable: gas estimates vary across nearby historical states for 46.0% of transactions on Base, compared to 13.9% on Ethereum, with especially high sensitivity for MEV and DeFi activity. Storage access patterns also diverge across states, limiting the effectiveness of access lists and complicating parallel execution.
  Our work provides an empirical foundation for multi-dimensional gas metering and explicit pricing of state growth. They show that state-sensitive execution behavior complicates workload estimation, directly affecting transaction predictability and user experience.

</details>


### [85] [Online Dynamic Batching with Formal Guarantees for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19989)
*Dian Li,Zekun Wang,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出在线动态批处理(ODB)系统，解决现代LLM训练中离线批处理采样器无法预知真实训练成本的问题，在保持分布式对齐的同时实现1.58-4.43倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM训练打破了离线批处理采样器的核心假设：样本的真实训练成本（包括预处理、数据增强、模板化、分词和多模态视觉token扩展等）仅在处理后方可观测。若不使用依赖预处理和增强的长度缓存，批构建将无法感知决定padding、内存使用和GPU饱和度的关键量，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 引入在线动态批处理(ODB)系统，这是一个DataLoader端的即插即用方案，将批构建移至可准确观测训练成本的时机，同时保持分布式数据并行(DDP)步骤对齐。形式化定义了分布式组对齐问题(DGAP)，证明在默认join模式恒等覆盖和可选非join样本配额闭包下可实现无死锁的有界终止。ODB无需修改模型、优化器或注意力核，仅通过轻量级训练器适配器即可集成。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL 2B/8B模型上的公开实验中：相比固定批处理标准方法，单节点Full FT/LoRA吞吐量提升1.58-2.51倍，双节点Full FT提升1.71-3.78倍；生产MM-Mix达到4.43倍加速。与GMT/BMT离线token预算预言机相比，在UltraChat/LLaVA上差距在15%以内，在高变异ShareGPT4o上更快：单节点Full FT/LoRA加速2.24-2.39倍，双节点Full FT加速3.06-3.69倍。质量与标准方法相当。

Conclusion: ODB成功填补了高异构性LLM微调场景中在线/即插即用方案的空白：在不牺牲训练质量的前提下实现大幅吞吐量提升，提供形式化DGAP保证，无需长度缓存预计算或核函数重写，为现代LLM训练提供高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern LLM training breaks a core assumption behind offline batch samplers: the true training cost of a sample is only observable after preprocessing, augmentation, templating, tokenization, and multimodal visual-token expansion. Unless one pays for a preprocessing- and augmentation-dependent length cache, batch construction is therefore blind to the quantity that determines padding, memory use, and GPU saturation. We introduce Online Dynamic Batching (ODB), a DataLoader-side drop-in system that moves batch formation to this point of accurate observability while preserving DDP step alignment. We formalize this synchronization requirement as the Distributed Group Alignment Problem and prove deadlock-free bounded termination with default join-mode identity coverage and opt-in non-join sample-quota closure. ODB requires no model, optimizer, or attention-kernel changes and is released as online-dynamic-batching with lightweight trainer adapters. Across public 2B/8B Qwen3-VL runs on UltraChat/LLaVA/ShareGPT4o, ODB improves literal emitted-sample throughput vs. fixed-batch Standard by 1.58-2.51x on single-node Full FT/LoRA and 1.71-3.78x on two-node Full FT, with Standard-comparable quality; production MM-Mix reaches 4.43x. Against GMT/BMT offline token-budget oracles, ODB is within 15% on UltraChat/LLaVA and faster on high-CV ShareGPT4o: 2.24-2.39x single-node Full FT/LoRA and 3.06-3.69x two-node Full FT. Together, ODB occupies the online/drop-in regime for high-heterogeneity LLM fine-tuning: large throughput gains at Standard-comparable quality, formal DGAP guarantees, and no length-cache precompute or kernel rewrites.

</details>


### [86] [ARGUS: Production-Scale Tracing and Performance Diagnosis for over 10,000-GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20374)
*Jiasheng Zhou,Longbin Zeng,Clavis Chen,Ruiming Lu,Qinwei Yang,Leyi Ye,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了ARGUS系统，在万卡级GPU生产集群中实现了开销低于2%的持续在线细粒度追踪与实时分析，数据压缩约3700倍，有效支持多种性能异常的检测与诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM训练需要持续、细粒度的可观测性以进行有效的性能诊断。现有粗粒度监控无法定位根因，而细粒度分析工具存在5%-30%的高开销和海量追踪数据问题，难以在大型生产集群中持续部署。

Method: ARGUS沿训练调用层次分解观测维度（CPU调用栈、框架语义、GPU内核执行），构建统一数据管道实现约3700倍的数据压缩；采用渐进式诊断框架，通过迭代级、阶段级和内核级分析自动隔离异常时间窗口、滞后节点和性能下降的内核。

Result: 在万卡级GPU生产集群部署超过六个月，持续在线收集开销低于2%；成功支持计算滞后节点、链路降级、流水线气泡放大、FlashAttention JIT停顿等多种异常检测与优化；能发现被通信症状掩盖的计算滞后问题。

Conclusion: ARGUS证明了低开销、细粒度、持续在线追踪在大规模生产环境中的可行性，为大模型训练提供了实用的性能诊断解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale LLM training requires always-on, fine-grained observability for effective performance diagnosis at scale. Coarse resource monitors alone cannot localize root causes, and fine-grained profilers incur prohibitive (5%-30%) overheads and massive trace volumes, making always-on deployment impractical in large production clusters.
  We propose ARGUS, a low-overhead, fine-grained, always-on tracing and real-time analysis system for training workloads in 10,000+ GPU-scale production clusters. ARGUS decomposes observation along the training call hierarchy into CPU call stacks, framework semantics, and GPU kernel execution, with always-on collection under a combined overhead of less than 2%. It builds a unified data pipeline and compresses raw kernel events by approximately 3,700x from 10 MB to 2.7 KB per rank per step. Its progressive diagnosis framework automatically isolates anomalous windows, straggler ranks, and degraded kernels through iteration-time, phase-level, and kernel-level analysis. Deployed for over six months on a 10,000+ GPU production cluster, ARGUS has supported continuous fail-slow detection and performance optimization. Our case studies further demonstrate its effectiveness across representative anomalies, including compute stragglers, link degradation, pipeline-bubble amplification, FlashAttention JIT stalls, and compute stragglers masked by communication symptoms.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“计算可识别性”框架，将传统的理论可识别性概念扩展到有限计算资源下的实际估计器搜索，通过实验展示了该框架在小样本、模糊图标准、混合数据等实际场景中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的因果可识别性研究假设渐近性质、无限数据或数学理想化条件，仅关注理论上的可识别性，而忽略了实际计算过程中的局限性。这种理论可识别性无法回答细粒度、实际的可识别性问题，例如有限小样本下的可识别性、图标准模糊时的可识别性、观测-干预混合数据下的可识别性，以及反事实数据和估计量跨场景的可识别性。因此，有必要建立一个能够处理实际计算约束的可识别性框架。

Method: 作者提出了“计算可识别性”框架，其核心思想是定义一个有限的计算搜索过程来寻找经验估计器。具体方法包括：(1) 将可识别性定义为在指定误差容忍度内通过搜索过程找到经验估计器；(2) 可识别性条件依赖于搜索过程的假设（如参数的先验分布）和搜索过程本身；(3) 提供了开源代码实现（https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify）。该框架将理论可识别性与计算约束相结合，使可识别性成为计算过程的结果而非纯理论判断。

Result: 通过多个实验验证了计算可识别性框架的有效性，展示了其能够处理：(1) 小有限样本情况下的可识别性判断；(2) 图标准模糊或不确定时的可识别性问题；(3) 观测数据与干预数据混合情况下的可识别性分析；(4) 反事实数据和估计量的跨场景可识别性问题。实验结果表明，该框架能够回答传统理论可识别性无法处理的细粒度实际问题。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性框架成功地将理论可识别性与实际计算约束相结合，为因果推断提供了更实用的可识别性判断方法。该框架突破了传统渐近理论的限制，能够在有限样本和复杂实际条件下进行可识别性分析，为因果推断的实际应用提供了新的理论工具和计算方法。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [88] [Low-Energy Reduced RISC-V Instruction Subset Processor for Tsetlin Machine Inference at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19964)
*Chanda Gupta,Sanidhya Bhatia,Shaurya Priyadarshi,Himani Panwar,Rishad Shafik,Sudip Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向Tsetlin Machine推理的领域专用RISC-V微处理器架构，通过指令精简和硬件优化，在保持可编程性的同时实现了高达98%的执行时间缩减和平均29.7倍的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Tsetlin Machine加速器设计虽然性能优异，但通常依赖于紧耦合接口、微码式编程和外部主机处理器，限制了系统的灵活性和编程便捷性。为解决这一问题，研究者希望设计一种既能保持可编程性又能针对TM工作负载优化性能和能耗的处理器架构。

Method: 利用RISC-V的模块化结构设计精简指令集处理器：首先通过指令性能分析指导指令精简，然后针对TM推理任务定制数据通路和控制通路的简化方案。该方法构建了基线RV32IM核心和精简核心，并与依赖位运算的二值化神经网络(BNN)进行对比评估。

Result: TM在多个数据集上实现了相当或更高的准确率（如CIFAR-2上88.18%对比BNN的60.0%），执行时间缩减高达98%，平均能耗降低29.7倍，证明了该方法在可编程边缘AI系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的面向TM推理的领域专用RISC-V架构成功平衡了可编程性与硬件效率，为边缘AI部署提供了一种高性能、低能耗且易于编程的解决方案。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machine (TM) is a logic-based machine learning approach that relies on simple bitwise operations and finite-state automata, which makes it attractive for edge AI deployments. Recent work has focused on co-processor and accelerator designs based on Tsetlin Machines (TMs). Although these designs achieve high performance, they typically depend on tightly coupled interfaces, microcode-style programming, and external host processors, limiting flexibility and ease of programming. In this work, we present a domain-specific RISC-V microprocessor architecture and design flow tailored for TM inference. Leveraging the modular structure of RISC-V, we design a reduced instruction subset processor that retains programmability while targeting improved performance and lower energy consumption for TM workloads. Instruction profiling is employed to guide instruction reduction, followed by datapath and control path simplifications tailored to TM inference. Both the baseline RV32IM core and the proposed reduced core are evaluated across multiple datasets and compared with Binarized Neural Networks (BNNs), which serve as a hardware-efficient baseline due to their reliance on bitwise operations during inference. Results show that TM achieves comparable or higher accuracy (e.g., up to 88.18% on CIFAR-2 compared to 60.0% for BNN) while reducing execution time by up to 98% across multiple datasets. Furthermore, the proposed design achieves an average $29.7\times$ reduction in energy consumption, demonstrating its effectiveness for programmable and efficient edge AI systems.

</details>


### [89] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GUARD框架，通过门控不确定性感知路由机制从分布错位的时间序列基础模型中提取知识，蒸馏训练轻量级专业预测器，实现边缘计算部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在物理科学应用中面临关键瓶颈：零样本应用时存在严重的分布错位问题，且计算成本过高，无法部署于边缘计算传感器网络。如何从错位的基础模型中提取潜在结构知识以训练轻量级专业预测器是核心挑战。

Method: 提出GUARD（门控不确定性感知路由蒸馏）框架，包含两个自适应机制：（1）上下文路由器：根据局部输入统计动态选择最相关教师，利用多样基础模型间的互补性；（2）不确定性门控温度机制：作为"断路器"，当教师置信度与域现实偏离时自动衰减蒸馏强度。

Result: 在气象、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个气候关键领域进行评估。相比固定权重多教师蒸馏基线，RMSE显著降低。即使教师因域间分布偏移导致零样本精度不佳，仍可成功提取知识。域错位教师在28.5%的最难实例上优于全局最优基础模型。

Conclusion: 该方法成功从分布错位的基础模型中蒸馏知识，实现高精度科学预测，适用于资源受限的边缘部署，为物理科学领域的时间序列预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [90] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPSD框架，利用边缘端4-bit量化小语言模型压缩用户提示词，在保持响应质量非劣性的前提下，平均每次调用节省99.9个输入token，估计节省70-270 uWh能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理的预填充阶段是云端能耗的重要来源。许多用户提示词包含礼貌标记、道歉性前言、重复和关系建立语言等社交性内容，这些内容对人类交流重要但对机器推理的信息边际贡献低，存在社交-语义鸿沟问题。

Method: 提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）边缘端流程：使用4-bit量化的小语言模型（Gemma-2-2B-Instruct Q4_K_M）在边缘端压缩用户提示词，然后传输至云端部署的LLM（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）；对安全关键领域通过基于规则的门控保守路由到直通路径。

Result: 在248提示词语料库上评估：平均每次蒸馏调用节省99.9个输入token，全部146次蒸馏调用均实现正向节省；121对盲评LLM-as-judge质量评估显示非劣性（15分标准下1分容差内），43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出；余弦相似度均值0.682、中位数0.712，54.1%超过0.70阈值；估计每次调用净能耗节省70-270 uWh。

Conclusion: SPSD证明了设备端提示词蒸馏能够在实际非劣性容差内保持响应质量的同时，有效降低云端LLM输入token成本，为减少LLM推理能耗提供了可行的边缘-云协同方案。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [91] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPICED方法，通过将少量人类驾驶数据（30分钟）作为正则化项与最小安全奖励结合，在自博弈强化学习中实现了与人类行为兼容的驾驶策略训练，数据需求仅为模仿学习的1/2500。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自博弈强化学习训练的驾驶策略虽然有效，但可能学习到与人类不兼容的异质驾驶习惯。现有方法通过大量奖励工程和域随机化来缓解行为偏差，但这种方法脆弱且劳动密集。

Method: 不完全抛弃人类示范数据，而是将少量人类数据（30分钟）作为正则化目标，叠加在最小安全目标达成奖励之上进行训练。方法在单块消费级GPU上完成15小时训练。

Result: 仅需30分钟人类示范数据（比可比模仿学习方法少2500倍），训练出的策略能够与留出的人类轨迹协调配合，训练时间15小时。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据在自博弈强化学习中起到关键调节作用，像调味料一样显著提升了策略与人类行为的兼容性，实现了数据高效、行为对齐的驾驶策略学习。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [92] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对医学扩散模型Med-DDPM进行了深入的性能分析，提出了TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两种架构感知优化，在A100上实现了高达100倍的SM周期和动态指令减少，并将Tensor Core利用率从1.45提升至9.98倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在高保真3D MRI合成中至关重要，但其部署受到巨大的GPU资源需求限制。每个样本需要数百次U-Net评估，且内核行为高度异构，导致计算开销巨大。为了解决这些性能瓶颈，有必要对最先进的医学扩散模型进行全面的性能分析。

Method: 在三代NVIDIA架构上对Med-DDPM进行全面的性能分析，包括内核级运行时分解、指令混合特性、内存系统利用率、warp级活动和性能分析器优先级评分估计。基于分析结果，提出了两种架构感知优化方法：TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局。

Result: 训练过程主要由cuDNN卷积和implicit-GEMM内核主导。识别出内存访问模式、张量布局转换和有限的Tensor Core利用率等效率瓶颈。优化后，SM周期减少高达100倍，动态指令减少100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45提升至9.98倍，A100上的IPC提高7%，且不降低合成质量。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的性能分析和架构感知优化，可以显著提升医学扩散模型的计算效率。TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局等优化方法能够在不牺牲生成质量的前提下，大幅降低计算开销，为高性能MRI合成提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [93] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型（PGM）之间的理论联系，证明ILL学习到的概率规则可解释为商变量上的约束型因子图，揭示了ILL与最大熵模型的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息格学习（ILL）通过交替投影学习信号的可解释规则，但当信号为概率质量函数时，学习到的概率规则缺乏清晰的概率图模型解释。本文旨在建立ILL与概率图模型之间的理论桥梁，以更好地理解ILL的本质及其与其他模型的关系。

Method: 将ILL的划分解释为确定性商变量，规则解释为商变量的边缘分布；将规则集解释为可解释抽象上的边缘约束集合；提出一般提升（满足约束的联合分布族）和特殊提升（最大无知重建，通过L2均匀性原理实现）；在香农熵提升下，将约束转化为对数线性因子图。

Result: 证明了ILL学习的概率规则具有自然的概率图模型解释：划分诱导商变量，规则是商变量的边缘律，规则集是边缘约束集合；ILL的特殊提升通过L2均匀性原理实现最大无知重建，与最大熵密切相关；在香农熵提升下，约束产生对数线性因子图，其因子由学习到的抽象索引；信息格本身不是贝叶斯网络，其边编码抽象的细化和粗化而非条件依赖。

Conclusion: ILL最好被理解为商变量上可解释的基于约束的因子图的结构学习，这一观点阐明了ILL与图模型和最大熵模型的关系，并为推理、可识别性和混合符号-概率学习的新方向提供了启示。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [94] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于Weibull框架研究了AdamW训练过程中权重尺度参数λ的“增长-过冲-弛豫”动态，通过三力分解揭示了其物理机制，并提出了从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力的样条位移方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解AdamW训练过程中Weibull权重尺度参数λ为何呈现增长、过冲然后弛豫的行为模式，需要深入分析其背后的动力学机制。

Method: 从AdamW更新推导出权重平方范数的首阶三力分解：对齐力（权重与自适应更新方向的相关性）、注入力（自适应步长）和衰减力（解耦权重衰减）；使用自训练的Pythia-70M模型验证；提出样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 对齐力主导增长阶段，贡献88-94%的绝对力预算；接近饱和时对齐力与衰减力趋于平衡，解释了从增长到弛豫的转变；样条位移方法恢复对齐力的准确率约92-94%，是朴素两点基线的两倍；RMS到Weibull重建偏移约5-6%；λ(t)峰值与训练数据连贯性相关。

Conclusion: 通过三力分解成功解释了权重尺度动态的物理机制，所提出的方法可扩展至真实模型分析，为理解优化器行为提供了新的理论视角和分析工具。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [95] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于多元零膨胀高斯分布(ZIG)的分布估计算法(EDA)，首次将EDA成功推广到稀疏优化领域，在Lunar Lander基准测试中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布估计算法(EDA)在连续参数空间上表现优异，但尚未推广到稀疏空间。现有的稀疏黑盒优化器引入了EDA原本要避免的问题：手工设计的稀疏算子、双层方案、零化阈值等内置假设。因此需要一种无需层次结构、能联合优化稀疏模式和活跃值的EDA方法。

Method: 提出多元零膨胀高斯(ZIG)分布作为EDA采样律。该模型包含分离的指示器和数值维度，通过潜在高斯模型表示稀疏模式、活跃参数间的相关性以及两者之间的交互作用，实现了稀疏模式和活跃值的联合优化。证明了该模型的潜在参数可识别性，并引入了实用的基于摊销逆变换的估计器。

Result: 估计器能准确恢复潜在相关结构；在Lunar Lander基准测试中，ZIG-EDA比稠密高斯EDA、手工设计的稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA收敛更快、最终回报更高，同时找到的控制器只有少量参数活跃。

Conclusion: ZIG-EDA成功填补了EDA在稀疏优化领域的空白，避免了手工设计算子的偏见，实现了稀疏模式和活跃值的联合优化，为稀疏黑盒优化提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [96] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ProMUSE，一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分级证据网络，通过基于Dirichlet主观逻辑量化不确定性，自适应决定何时需要引入昂贵成像模态（MRI/PET），在保持诊断准确率的同时减少50-90%的影像使用，实现成本效益高的阿尔茨海默病早期诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病（AD）早期诊断需求迫切，现有方法依赖多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但MRI和PET成像成本高昂且普及性差，导致全模态推断在真实临床场景中不切实际，亟需一种在保持诊断精度的同时降低数据获取成本的方法。

Method: 提出ProMUSE（渐进式多模态不确定性引导分级证据网络）：首先利用低成本临床数据进行证据分类，通过基于Dirichlet的主观逻辑模型量化不确定性；当不确定性超过学习阈值时，渐进式引入MRI或PET特征；采用Dempster-Shafer理论融合各模态的置信度和不确定性，获得校准的多模态预测，实现分级采集策略。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD任务中，ProMUSE相比全模态基线达到竞争性或更优的准确率，同时减少50-90%的MRI/PET使用率，显著降低成本。

Conclusion: ProMUSE是一种实用、具备不确定性感知、资源高效的解决方案，适用于真实世界的AD筛查，在保证诊断精度的前提下大幅降低医疗资源消耗。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

</details>


### [97] [Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20493)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Contagion Networks框架，量化LLM评估者偏见在多智能体系统中的传播。实验发现同模型智能体间的偏见传播系数(0.157-0.352)比跨模型低3-5倍，且将评估委员会从1增至3个成员可减少72.4%的有效传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型作为评估者的多智能体系统中，系统性的评估偏见会在智能体网络中传播，但目前缺乏量化这种偏见传播机制的正式框架。

Method: 提出Contagion Networks形式化框架，通过控制性3智能体实验（使用DeepSeek-chat和三种不同评估者偏见配置），测量跨智能体传染矩阵Gamma_3，分析由谱半径rho(Gamma_N)控制的传播机制。

Result: 发现评估者偏见在智能体间持续传播（gamma在[0.157, 0.352]范围）；识别出三种传播机制；同模型智能体的传染系数比先前研究的跨模型系数（MM-EPC: gamma约0.85-1.3）弱3-5倍；将评估委员会规模从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。

Conclusion: 同模型智能体系统处于抑制机制，偏见传播较弱；增加评估委员会规模是可行的缓解策略；开源了Contagion Network实验框架供研究使用。

Abstract: When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

</details>


### [98] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出cAPM——一种持续AI辅助的起搏标测方法，通过任务无关的代理神经网络和持续学习策略积累跨目标知识，仅需4.5个起搏位点即可在5mm临床容差内实现81%的定位概率，显著优于现有方法的13.7个位点38%概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室性心动过速(VT)是危及生命的心律失常，起搏标测是导管消融中确定干预靶点的关键临床程序。现有主动学习AI模型虽能减少起搏位点数量，但存在关键局限：每个VT目标都需要重新训练，无法在同一患者的多个VT或跨患者之间迁移知识。这导致知识无法累积，每次处理新目标都从零开始，效率低下。

Method: cAPM采用三大核心技术：1)任务无关的代理神经网络，学习从起搏位点到12导联ECG形态的映射关系；2)主动学习策略，通过选择每个目标信息量最大的起搏位点来优化代理模型；3)持续学习策略，按序处理目标同时保留先验目标的知识。系统在包含不同生理条件和心室几何形状的顺序定位任务中进行评估，支持有/无历史数据回放两种模式。

Result: 在in-silico测试平台上，cAPM（无论是否回放历史数据）仅用4.5个起搏位点即在5mm临床容差内达到81%的定位成功率，相比之下，最先进的主动学习方法使用13.7个起搏位点仅达到38%的成功率。cAPM在保持更高精度的同时将所需起搏位点数量减少约67%。

Conclusion: cAPM为AI辅助起搏标测提供了强有力的持续学习框架，通过跨目标知识迁移显著提升了定位效率和准确性。研究成果为推进cAPM进入体内临床前研究和临床应用奠定了坚实基础，有望在真实临床环境中指导起搏标测，提高室性心动过速导管消融的治疗效果。

Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

</details>


### [99] [Execution-State Capsules: Graph-Bound Execution-State Checkpoint and Restore for Low-Latency, Small-Batch, On-Device Physical-AI Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20537)
*Liang Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Execution-State Capsules机制，针对低延迟、小批量的端侧物理AI推理场景，通过图绑定的检查点与恢复机制管理完整执行状态，实现比传统KV缓存更高效的复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流LLM服务系统通过分页或基数KV缓存重用前缀工作负载，适合高吞吐高并发场景。但在交互式LLM代理、语音系统、机器人策略等端侧低延迟、小批量的物理AI服务中，系统经常需要分支、重置、中断和重新进入，现有方法仅管理KV缓存这一位置片段，无法满足紧响应约束。

Method: 引入Execution-State Capsules：图绑定的检查点与恢复机制，管理提交边界处的完整可恢复状态。FlashRT是面向后端的白盒内核运行时，使用NVIDIA CUDA后端在连续静态缓冲区上执行捕获的图计划，无块表间接寻址。由于活动状态是命名缓冲区的闭集，capsule可以快照、恢复、分支或回滚整个执行边界，包括KV、循环状态、卷积状态、MTP状态和元数据。

Result: 在RTX 5090上，capsule恢复在存储状态级别字节精确，贪婪解码下token相同；仅KV的消融实验产生分歧，证明循环状态具有承载作用。GPU驻留快照和恢复在亚毫秒级；相比冷预填充，TTFT加速从2k token的3.9x增长到16k token的27x。在Jetson AGX Thor和DGX Spark上验证了相同的正确性和结构特性。

Conclusion: Capsules不是高吞吐KV缓存服务的替代品，而是定义了一个互补的延迟优先服务点，用于显式执行状态重用，为低延迟端侧物理AI推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Mainstream LLM serving systems reuse prefix work mainly through paged or radix key-value (KV) caches. This is highly effective for high-throughput, high-concurrency serving, but it manages only one positional fragment of execution state: the KV cache. We study the opposite regime: low-latency, small-batch, on-device physical-AI serving, where interactive LLM agents, speech systems, and robot policies repeatedly branch, reset, interrupt, and re-enter under tight responsiveness budgets. We introduce execution-state capsules, a graph-bound checkpoint and restore mechanism for the complete restorable state at a committed boundary. FlashRT is a white-box, backend-facing kernel runtime whose evaluated NVIDIA CUDA backend runs captured graph plans over contiguous static buffers with no block-table indirection. Because the live state is a closed set of named buffers, a capsule can snapshot, restore, fork, or roll back the whole execution boundary, including KV, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. This moves reuse from token-addressed KV fragments to graph-bound execution-state boundaries. On an RTX 5090, capsule restore is byte-exact at the stored-state level and token-identical under greedy decode. A KV-only ablation diverges, showing that recurrent state is load-bearing. GPU-resident snapshot and restore are sub-millisecond, and TTFT speedup over cold prefill grows from 3.9x at 2k tokens to 27x at 16k tokens. On Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark, the same correctness and structural properties hold. Capsules are not a replacement for high-throughput KV-cache serving; they define a complementary latency-first serving point for explicit execution-state reuse.

</details>


### [100] [Protein Representation Learning with Secondary-Structure and Energy-Filtered Hydrogen-Bond Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19374)
*Mohamed Mouhajir,Limei Wang,El Houcine Bergou,Hajar El Hammouti,Lamiae Azizi,Dongqi Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于二级结构感知的图神经网络，通过整合蛋白质二级结构信息和能量过滤的氢键相互作用来构建图拓扑，在蛋白质表示学习任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质图建模方法主要依赖序列邻接关系或几何邻近性，仅部分反映蛋白质折叠原理。蛋白质实际采用围绕二级结构元素（α螺旋和β折叠）组织的复杂三维构象，这些元素编码了重复的局部基序和稳定的氢键相互作用。

Method: 设计二级结构感知的图神经网络：残基级节点表示增强二级结构分配信息；图边由按能量强度过滤的氢键相互作用构建；该设计使模型能够捕获对蛋白质稳定性和功能至关重要的局部结构上下文和长程耦合。

Result: 在常用蛋白质基准测试上相比现有图方法取得一致性改进；生成的图表示具有增强的生物可解释性，学习到的连接性与已建立的结构基序一致。

Conclusion: 整合二级结构和能量过滤的氢键拓扑为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions. In this work, we introduce a secondary-structure-aware graph neural network for protein representation learning. Residue-level node representations are augmented with secondary structure assignments, and graph edges are constructed from hydrogen-bond interactions filtered by their energetic strength. This design enables the model to capture both local structural context and long-range couplings that are central to protein stability and function. We evaluate the proposed approach on commonly used protein benchmarks and observe consistent improvements over existing graph-based methods. In addition, the resulting graph representations offer enhanced biological interpretability, as the learned connectivity aligns with established structural motifs. These findings suggest that incorporating secondary structure and energy-filtered hydrogen-bond topology provides an effective inductive bias for protein representation learning. The code is released at https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

</details>


### [101] [Physics-Informed Discovery of Yield Functions in Plasticity via Convex Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19375)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Donghyuk Cho,Jecheon Yu,Jeong Whan Yoon,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息驱动的框架，可从全场位移数据和反力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量或预设参数化形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别各向异性屈服函数面临多重挑战：屈服无法直接从全场力学测量中观测，方向标定需要大量加载方向，且选择合适的解析形式非常困难。

Method: 将屈服函数表示为凸神经网络，强制执行凸性和一阶正齐次性并施加拉压对称假设；采用可微分应力更新和物理信息力平衡损失，在多载荷工况下训练该神经屈服函数，将其作为弹塑性应力积分内部受力学约束的本构分量进行识别。

Result: 利用von Mises、Hill 1948和Yld2000-2d屈服函数的有限元基准研究验证了框架有效性，评估内容包括屈服轮廓一致性、位移噪声敏感性、通过塑性活跃应力状态的可识别性、认知不确定性及多项式代理部署。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了一条力学约束的路径，能够从位移和力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，同时保持所识别分量处于弹塑性应力积分结构之内。

Abstract: Identifying anisotropic yield functions remains challenging since yielding is not directly observed in full-field mechanical measurements, directional calibration can require many loading directions, and selecting an appropriate analytical form is nontrivial. This study proposes a physics-informed framework for discovering yield functions from full-field displacement data and reaction force data, without stress observations, plastic strain measurements, direct yield surface data, or a prescribed parametric yield function. The framework identifies the yield function as a mechanically constrained constitutive component inside elastoplastic stress integration, rather than through direct stress-space supervision. The yield function is represented by a convex neural network that enforces convexity and positive homogeneity of degree one while imposing the assumed tension-compression symmetry, and this neural yield function is trained with a differentiable stress update and a physics-informed force equilibrium loss across multiple loading cases. The proposed framework is validated using finite element (FE) benchmark studies with von Mises, Hill 1948, and Yld2000-2d yield functions, assessing yield contour agreement, displacement-noise sensitivity, identifiability through plastically active stress states, epistemic uncertainty, and polynomial-surrogate deployment. This study provides a mechanics-constrained pathway for discovering anisotropic yield functions from displacement and force data while keeping the identified component within the structure of elastoplastic stress integration.

</details>


### [102] [Cost-Optimal LLM Routing with Limited User Feedback under User Satisfaction Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19376)
*Herbert Woisetschläger,Arastun Mammadli,Ryan Zhang,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SLARouter，一种在线LLM请求路由算法，利用生产环境中稀疏的单边用户反馈学习成本最优策略，在保证严格SLA合规的同时将运营成本降低高达2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型应用的推理成本快速增长，用户期望的高质量响应与SLA要求形成了成本与质量之间的根本矛盾。现有的成本感知LLM请求路由方法依赖完整反馈信号、离线训练、大量针对特定工作负载的调优，且缺乏SLA保证或推理时自适应能力。

Method: 提出SLARouter在线路由算法，从生产系统中可用的稀疏、单边用户反馈中学习成本最优策略，并提供成本最优性和严格SLA合规性的理论保证。该方法无需针对特定基准测试进行调优。

Result: 在广泛的LLM基准测试中，SLARouter满足SLA约束，且无需针对每个基准测试进行调优，相比现有基线方法将运营成本降低高达2.2倍。

Conclusion: SLARouter成功解决了LLM推理中成本与质量之间的矛盾，通过在线学习稀疏反馈实现了成本最优路由，同时保证了SLA合规性，为生产环境中的LLM应用提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Inference costs for large language model (LLM) applications are rapidly growing, driven by surging demand and rising infrastructure cost. Users expect high-quality responses, and in commercial settings this is formally codified in Service Level Agreements (SLAs), creating a fundamental tension between cost and quality. Recent progress on cost-aware LLM request routing has shown potential to resolve this tension, but existing approaches rely on complete feedback signals, offline training, extensive per-workload tuning, and most lack SLA guarantees or inference-time adaptivity. We introduce SLARouter, an online routing algorithm that learns a cost-optimal policy from the sparse, one-sided user feedback available in production systems. SLARouter provides theoretical guarantees for both cost optimality and strict SLA compliance. Experiments across a wide range of LLM benchmarks show that SLARouter satisfies SLA constraints without the need for per-benchmark tuning, reducing operating cost by up to 2.2x over existing baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Emyx: Fast and efficient all-atom protein generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19377)
*Nicholas J. Williams,Ward Haddadin,Matteo P. Ferla,Constantin Schneider,Nicholas B. Woodall,Ruby Sedgwick,Christian D. Madsen,Andrew L. Hopkins,Edward O. Pyzer-Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Emyx是一个140M参数的条件流匹配模型，通过轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接替代传统架构中的重型嵌入堆栈，在酶设计任务上以最小模型尺寸超越现有方法，同时训练成本降低4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全原子生成模型继承了蛋白质结构预测的昂贵架构，导致训练成本高且样本多样性有限。作者认为生成器仅需处理稀疏几何约束而非丰富的共进化信号，因此传统架构的复杂性对生成任务是不必要的。

Method: 提出Emyx模型：1) 140M参数的条件流匹配模型，将容量集中在标准transformer块中；2) 用轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接替代重型嵌入堆栈；3) 推导出流匹配插值的精确重参数化到EDM噪声级框架，将流匹配训练效率与扩散模型的最先进采样方法桥接。

Result: 在AME酶设计基准测试中，Emyx在成功率（需同时满足全局折叠恢复和催化几何精度）、结构新颖性、支架多样性和几何有效性方面均超越Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3，尽管模型参数最小。训练仅需682 GPU小时，比RFdiffusion3少约4倍。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了针对生成任务设计的专用架构可以显著降低计算成本并提升性能，为计算酶设计领域提供了一个更高效的解决方案，挑战了继承自结构预测模型的重型架构范式。

Abstract: Computational enzyme design requires generating proteins that scaffold catalytic residues and ligands, a task that demands both geometric accuracy and structural diversity from the underlying generative model. Current all-atom generators inherit expensive architectures from structure prediction, leading to high training costs and limited sample diversity. We argue that much of this complexity is unnecessary for generators, which condition on sparse geometric constraints rather than rich co-evolutionary signals. Emyx is a 140M-parameter conditional flow matching model that concentrates capacity within standard transformer blocks, replacing heavy embedding stacks with lightweight conditional representations and sparse connectivity. We additionally derive an exact reparametrisation of the flow matching interpolant into the EDM noise-level framework, bridging flow matching training efficiency with state-of-the-art sampling methods designed for diffusion models without retraining. Despite being the smallest model, Emyx outperforms both Proteína-Complexa and RFdiffusion3 against the AME enzyme design benchmark across success rate under strict evaluation requiring both global fold recovery and catalytic geometry accuracy, structural novelty, scaffold diversity, and geometric validity, while training in just $682$ GPU-hours, roughly $4\times$ less than RFdiffusion3.

</details>


### [104] [A Hybrid GNN-FEM Framework for Phase-Field Fracture Simulation. Physics-Preserving Hybridization for Generalizable Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19378)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Yongjin Choi,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合图神经网络-有限元方法(GNN-FEM)框架，通过用GNN代理模型替代相场断裂模型中的相场更新步骤，在保持物理一致性和泛化能力的同时，显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场方法为模拟复杂裂纹演化提供了稳健的变分框架，但由于需要在增量有限元过程中求解耦合、非线性和历史相关的系统，其高昂的计算成本限制了实际应用。现有SciML方法在处理非线性、历史相关问题时难以保证物理一致性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出混合GNN-FEM框架：将图神经网络代理模型集成到传统的交错方案中，在每个载荷增量步用GNN替代相场更新，同时保留基于FEM的位移求解器以强制执行机械平衡和边界条件。通过保留增量解结构，框架与历史相关的裂纹演化保持一致。采用无量纲特征设计、基于网格区域的图表述以及从控制相场方程导出的物理信息损失函数来增强泛化能力。

Result: 数值实验表明，该混合方法相比传统FEM显著降低了计算成本，同时保持了准确性；在不同几何形状、加载条件、材料属性和离散化方案下均表现出强泛化能力和稳健的预测性能。

Conclusion: 选择性代理策略（仅替代相场更新而非整个求解轨迹）能够有效识别物理上有意义且增量结构化的学习目标，避免了暴力数据生成，实现了高效、准确且可泛化的相场断裂建模。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning (SciML) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating simulations of complex physical systems, yet achieving physically consistent and generalizable predictions for nonlinear, history-dependent problems remains a central challenge. In this study, we propose a hybrid GNN--FEM framework for efficient and generalizable phase-field fracture modeling. While phase-field approaches provide a robust variational framework for simulating complex crack evolution, their high computational cost limits practical applications because they require solving coupled, nonlinear, and history-dependent systems within an incremental finite element procedure. To address this challenge, a graph neural network surrogate is integrated into the conventional staggered scheme, replacing the phase-field update at each load increment while retaining the FEM-based displacement solver to enforce mechanical equilibrium and boundary conditions. By preserving the incremental solution structure, the framework remains consistent with history-dependent fracture evolution without requiring the surrogate to approximate the full solution trajectory. This selective surrogate strategy emphasizes the identification of a physically meaningful and incrementally structured learning target, rather than relying on brute-force data generation to learn the full fracture process. The proposed framework achieves strong generalization across varying geometries, loading conditions, material properties, and discretizations through dimensionless feature design, a graph-based formulation on mesh-based domains, and a physics-informed loss derived from the governing phase-field equation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the hybrid approach reduces computational cost while maintaining accuracy compared with conventional FEM, and exhibits robust predictive performance across diverse problem settings.

</details>


### [105] [How Linear Is a Transformer Feed-Forward Block? Per-Block Linear Recoverability Is Learned, Not Architectural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19379)
*Stuart Whipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化无关的线性可恢复性指标（R²_lin）来测量Transformer中FFN块的实际非线性程度，发现不同块之间存在显著异质性，可用于指导模型压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的前馈网络（FFN）通常被视为非线性的计算存储单元，但训练后的FFN块实际上具有多大的非线性程度却很少被测量。研究者希望了解这些块的真实非线性特性，而非仅仅依赖架构层面的假设。

Method: 将每个FFN视为位置级的输入-输出映射，将其分解为精确的最小二乘线性近似加上残差。通过闭式线性映射解释的保留方差定义块的线性可恢复性（R²_lin），这是一个优化无关的线性度度量指标。同时使用低秩双线性探针分析残差，并报告精确的闭式最小二乘上限。

Result: 在GPT-2、Pythia-160m和llama-160m的所有12个块中，R²_lin在深度上呈现高度异质性和非单调性，相邻块之间可从接近线性（>0.99）到强非线性（<0.3）。这种特性不是由激活函数决定的：相同宽度的GELU模型GPT-2和Pythia-160m具有截然不同的特征，说明可恢复性是训练块的习得属性而非架构属性。低秩双线性探针仅能恢复少量R²，且增益与残差非线性无关。

Conclusion: 线性可恢复性是训练过程中学习的个体块属性，可用于指导目标压缩：高可恢复性块可以用参数减少8倍的单层替换（困惑度仅增加0.77），而低可恢复性块则表明压缩不安全。研究还揭示了方法学陷阱：训练的线性基线在病态的Transformer激活上可能严重欠收敛，因此应报告精确的闭式最小二乘上限。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward networks (FFNs) are often treated as nonlinear stores of computation, yet how nonlinear a trained FFN block actually is has rarely been measured. We treat each FFN as a position-wise input-to-output map and split it into the exact least-squares linear approximation plus a residual. The held-out variance the closed-form linear map explains defines a block's linear recoverability (R^2_lin), an optimiser-free measure of its linearity.
  Across all twelve blocks of GPT-2, Pythia-160m, and llama-160m, R^2_lin is highly heterogeneous and non-monotone with depth, ranging from near-linear (>0.99) to strongly nonlinear (<0.3) between adjacent blocks, and is not set by the activation function: same-width GELU models GPT-2 and Pythia-160m have sharply different profiles, so recoverability is a learned property of individual trained blocks, not an architectural one. A low-rank bilinear probe of the residual recovers only a few points of R^2, with gain uncorrelated with residual nonlinearity: the unrecovered computation is not a single position-wise product but higher-order or distributed structure.
  The measurement also serves as a targeted compression signal: recoverable blocks admit large single-layer replacements (GPT-2's early FFN at 8x fewer parameters for +0.77 perplexity), while low-recoverability blocks flag where this is unsafe. It further exposes a methodological pitfall: trained linear baselines can badly under-converge on ill-conditioned transformer activations, so we report the exact closed-form least-squares ceiling throughout.

</details>


### [106] [VERITAS: Verifier-Guided Proof Search for Zero-Shot Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19399)
*Manish Acharya,Zhenyu Liao,Yueke Zhang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了VERITAS框架，通过两阶段协议将验证器的丰富信号（语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）反馈回证明搜索中，在miniF2F上达到40.6%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的形式化证明器通常将验证器的丰富信号（如语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）简化为二元的通过/失败信号，导致宝贵的信息丢失，限制了证明搜索的效率和效果。

Method: VERITAS采用两阶段零样本框架：第一阶段进行Best-of-N采样；第二阶段进行评论家引导的MCTS搜索，将第一阶段的失败作为显式的负例输入。该协议保留了第一阶段解决的所有定理，使第二阶段的额外解决可归因于反馈驱动的探索。

Result: VERITAS在miniF2F基准上达到40.6%的准确率（相比独立运行的Best-of-5为36.9%，Portfolio为26.2%）；在发布的55个定理的组合数学基准VERITAS-CombiBench上达到7.3%（Best-of-5为1.8%，Portfolio为3.6%），揭示了在需要从验证器反馈迭代恢复正确引理名称时，无引导采样的局限性。

Conclusion: 通过有效利用验证器的细粒度反馈信号，VERITAS显著提升了LLM在形式化定理证明中的性能，特别是在需要迭代推理的复杂场景中表现突出。研究同时发布了新的组合数学基准以促进相关研究。

Abstract: LLM-based formal provers often collapse rich verifier signals (syntax errors, type mismatches, partial goal progress) into a binary pass/fail bit. We present VERITAS, a zero-shot framework that routes every verifier signal back into proof search through a two-phase protocol: Best-of-N sampling first, then a critic-guided MCTS pass that ingests Phase 1 failures as explicit negative examples. The protocol preserves every theorem solved by its own Phase 1 sweep, so Phase 2's additional solves are attributable to feedback-driven exploration. VERITAS reaches 40.6% on miniF2F (vs. an independently run Best-of-5 at 36.9%, Portfolio 26.2%) and 7.3% on VERITAS-CombiBench, a 55-theorem combinatorics benchmark we release on which Best-of-5 (1.8%) falls below Portfolio (3.6%), exposing that unguided sampling hurts when correct lemma names must be recovered iteratively from verifier feedback. Artifacts are available on GitHub.

</details>


### [107] [Thermodynamic Signatures of Reasoning: Free-Energy and Spectral-Form-Factor Diagnostics for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19404)
*Salim Khazem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自由能特征(Fes)作为大语言模型的幻觉检测方法，通过将注意力拉普拉斯算子视为哈密顿量提取热力学势，在6个LLM和6个基准测试上取得最优AUROC，相比LapEig提升6.5分，无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力拉普拉斯谱的幻觉检测方法仅使用少量特征值或手工标量总结谱结构，浪费了大部分有用信息，需要更充分利用谱结构的方法。

Method: 将每层注意力拉普拉斯算子视为哈密顿量，提取热力学势（配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容）和随机矩阵理论谱形式因子，构建Fes谱描述符；证明了Lipschitz稳定性、表达能力增强和有限样本PAC界；同时提出RMT偏差分数作为无监督检测器。

Result: 在6个开源LLM和6个基准测试上，Fes轻量探测在注意力谱基线中取得最强综合AUROC，比LapEig高6.5分，比GoR-4高2.4分；无监督RMT偏差分数达平均AUROC 0.71；正确生成呈现Wigner-Dyson类统计，幻觉呈现Poisson类统计。

Conclusion: Fes通过热力学框架充分利用注意力拉普拉斯谱结构，为LLM幻觉检测提供了理论基础扎实、性能优异且无需更新底层模型的解决方案，谱统计特性可作为幻觉的有力指标。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) is deployment-critical, and recent work shows that the spectrum of attention-derived graph Laplacians carries strong signal about reasoning quality. Prior spectral diagnostics, however, summarize the Laplacian spectrum by a handful of eigenvalues or hand-picked scalars, leaving most of its structure unused. We propose Free-Energy Signatures (Fes), a spectral descriptor that treats each layer's attention Laplacian as a Hamiltonian and extracts its thermodynamic potentials partition function, free energy, spectral entropy, heat capacity together with the random-matrix-theory (RMT) spectral form factor.
